2026-11-24T08:35:52.4362284Z set -euo pipefail
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4362544Z SOURCE_SHA="$GITHUB_SHA"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4362809Z SHORT_SHA="${SOURCE_SHA::7}"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4363076Z PUBLISH_MODE="none"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4363313Z PR_IMAGE_TAG=""
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4363539Z PUSH_OUTPUT="false"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4363774Z 
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4364078Z # workflow_call path (called from integration.yml). The planner only
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4364539Z # invokes us when a build is actually needed (no pin/cache match), so
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4364979Z # we always publish. The publish mode follows the caller's event:
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4365431Z # pull_request -> pr-scoped image (consumed by the calling Integration
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4365884Z # run via digest output, not by tag polling); main/schedule/dispatch
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4366379Z # -> pin-refresh image (operator opens a reviewed image-pin.json PR
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4366736Z # afterwards).
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4367002Z if [[ -n "$EXPECTED_FINGERPRINT" ]]; then
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4367296Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4367676Z   if [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "pull_request" && "$PR_HEAD_REPO" == "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" ]]; then
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4368179Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pr"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4368491Z     PR_IMAGE_TAG="${IMAGE_NAME}:pr-${PR_NUMBER}-${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4368904Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4369255Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4369832Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "schedule" || "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4370223Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4370458Z   else
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4370753Z     # Untrusted PR (fork) reaching workflow_call should have been
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4371143Z     # blocked at the planner; defend in depth here too.
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4371653Z     echo "::error::workflow_call invoked from unsupported event ($EVENT_NAME, head_repo=$PR_HEAD_REPO)." >&2
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4372121Z     exit 1
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4372328Z   fi
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4372629Z # Standalone path: push to main, weekly schedule, or manual dispatch.
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4373076Z # These refresh the reviewed image-pin.json digest. The pull_request
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4373511Z # trigger no longer exists on this workflow; PR builds reach us only
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4373891Z # through the workflow_call branch above.
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4374257Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4374616Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4374853Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4375122Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "schedule" ]]; then
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4375409Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4375651Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4375961Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4376288Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="${DISPATCH_PUSH}"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4376602Z   if [[ "$DISPATCH_PUSH" == "true" ]]; then
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4376949Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4377259Z   fi
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4377466Z fi
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4377666Z {
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4377884Z   echo "source_sha=${SOURCE_SHA}"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4378184Z   echo "short_sha=${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4378493Z   echo "cache_key=ijt-browser-ci-${SOURCE_SHA}"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4378794Z   echo "push=${PUSH_OUTPUT}"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4379194Z   echo "publish_mode=${PUBLISH_MODE}"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4379638Z   echo "pr_image_tag=${PR_IMAGE_TAG}"
2026-11-24T08:35:52.4379933Z } >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
